## LLM Infrastructure

### [v0.20.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.20.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.20.0</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 546 commits from 257 contributors (83 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>CUDA 13.0 default</strong>: Default CUDA wheel switched to CUDA 13.0; architecture lists and build-args cleaned up (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39878">#39878</a>). As a general rule of thumb, our CUDA version policy follows PyTorch's.</li>
<li><strong>PyTorch 2.11 upgrade</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34644">#34644</a>): vLLM ships on torch 2.11 for CUDA; XPU temporarily remains on torch-xpu 2.10 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39656">#39656</a>). This is a breaking change for environment dependency.</li>
<li><strong>Transformers v5</strong>: vLLM now runs on HuggingFace <code>transformers&gt;=5</code> (landed in v0.19.1); v0.20.0 carries continued v4/v5 compat fixes including PaddleOCR-VL image processor <code>max_pixels</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38629">#38629</a>) and Mistral YaRN warning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37292">#37292</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlashAttention 4 as default MLA prefill</strong>: FA4 re-enabled as the default MLA prefill backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38819">#38819</a>) with head-dim 512 and paged-KV support on SM90+ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38835">#38835</a>), plus an upstream FA4 sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38690">#38690</a>).</li>
<li><strong>TurboQuant 2-bit KV cache</strong>: New attention backend delivering 2-bit KV cache compression with 4× capacity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38479">#38479</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Online quantization frontend</strong>: New end-to-end online quantization frontend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38138">#38138</a>), with docs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39736">#39736</a>); experts_int8 consolidated into the FP8 online path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38463">#38463</a>).</li>
<li><strong>vLLM IR</strong>: Initial IR skeleton with rms_norm op (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33825">#33825</a>), OOT-platform kernel imports (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38807">#38807</a>), and gemma_rms_norm reworked on IR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39014">#39014</a>) — foundation for future kernel work.</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 advances</strong>: Eagle prefill full-CUDA-graph (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37588">#37588</a>), auto-resolve cudagraph mode/sizes from attention backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32936">#32936</a>), fused probabilistic rejection sample kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38496">#38496</a>), config validation for unsupported features (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38758">#38758</a>), and piecewise-fallback disabled for eagle draft decodes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39773">#39773</a>).</li>
<li><strong>MoE refactor series</strong>: Unquantized migrated to Full Oracle Flow (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36286">#36286</a>), SharedExperts class (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35153">#35153</a>), DefaultMoERunner split (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35326">#35326</a>), ZeroExpertFusedMoE in new framework (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35549">#35549</a>), compressed_tensors_moe.py split up (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38960">#38960</a>), and MoE DP chunking removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39107">#39107</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>New architectures: EXAONE-4.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39388">#39388</a>), BharatGen Param2MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38000">#38000</a>), Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38306">#38306</a>), Cheers multimodal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38788">#38788</a>), telechat3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38510">#38510</a>), FireRedLID (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39290">#39290</a>), jina-reranker-v3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38800">#38800</a>), Jina Embeddings v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39575">#39575</a>), Nemotron-v3 VL Nano/Super (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39747">#39747</a>).</li>
<li>Quantization formats: GGUF support for MiniMax-M2.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36965">#36965</a>), non-standard GGUF quant types with prefix such as UD-IQ1_S (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39471">#39471</a>).</li>
<li>Speculative decoding: Eagle3 for MiniMax-M2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37512">#37512</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA: Qwen3ASRForConditionalGeneration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37247">#37247</a>), Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39291">#39291</a>), dual-CUDA-streams linear layer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35721">#35721</a>).</li>
<li>Multimodal MRoPE refresh: mm_features-based MRoPE for Ernie-4.5 VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39753">#39753</a>), Keye-VL / Keye-1.5-VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39869">#39869</a>), PaddleOCR-VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39888">#39888</a>).</li>
<li>Parakeet UX / perf enhancements (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39423">#39423</a>); ColModernVBERT updated for latest HF checkpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39307">#39307</a>); NemotronH default <code>mamba_ssm_cache_dtype=float32</code> with NemotronHNanoVLV2 auto-hook (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39032">#39032</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: Full CUDA graph for eagle prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37588">#37588</a>), auto cudagraph mode/sizes based on attention backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32936">#32936</a>), fused probabilistic rejection-sample kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38496">#38496</a>), config validation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38758">#38758</a>), eagle-draft piecewise fallback disabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39773">#39773</a>).</li>
<li><strong>vLLM IR</strong>: IR skeleton + rms_norm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33825">#33825</a>), OOT kernel import hooks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38807">#38807</a>), gemma_rms_norm on IR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39014">#39014</a>).</li>
<li><strong>torch.compile</strong>: Opaque Objects on torch 2.11 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39286">#39286</a>), AOT compile with batch-invariance mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39201">#39201</a>), Inductor cache nested under AOT dir (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39718">#39718</a>), split FX graph via codegen (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38657">#38657</a>), Inductor pre-grad passes re-enabled for torch≥2.12 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38944">#38944</a>), strings in custom ops without compile regressions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38123">#38123</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Attention</strong>: FA4 as default MLA prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38819">#38819</a>), head-dim 512 + paged-KV on sm90+FA4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38835">#38835</a>), FA4 upstream sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38690">#38690</a>), full CUDA graph for FlexAttention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36298">#36298</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Helion kernels</strong>: torch.compile support for Helion kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38592">#38592</a>).</li>
<li><strong>HMA / KV offload</strong>: GPU-side KV events for HMA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37688">#37688</a>), group block hashes/IDs tracked (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37109">#37109</a>), unified memory layout for offloading workers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37206">#37206</a>), <code>shutdown()</code> on OffloadingConnector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39182">#39182</a>), request context passed through KV offload (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39185">#39185</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Features</strong>: NUMA binding for GPU workers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38635">#38635</a>), opt-in <code>VLLM_MEDIA_CACHE</code> media URL caching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37123">#37123</a>), safe request abort when FSM fails to advance (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38663">#38663</a>), KV connector prioritized over internal registry (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38301">#38301</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Pluggable layers</strong>: Applied to llm_head / vocab embedding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33465">#33465</a>) and MoE layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33556">#33556</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Mamba</strong>: Stochastic rounding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35753">#35753</a>), different Conv state layouts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37416">#37416</a>), FlashInfer <code>selective_state_update</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36162">#36162</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Metrics &amp; scheduling</strong>: Labeled waiting-breakdown (capacity/deferred) metric (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38435">#38435</a>), API server handshake simplified (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39364">#39364</a>), mm-scheduler <code>get_num_embed</code> overhead reduced (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40143">#40143</a>), <code>request_id</code> on <code>FinishedRequestStats</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39710">#39710</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Executor</strong>: RayExecutorV2 introduced (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36836">#36836</a>); unified engine process monitoring with Ray backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35862">#35862</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>: swapAB support for SM120 CUTLASS blockwise FP8 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38325">#38325</a>), MXFP4 W4A4 CUTLASS MoE for SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37463">#37463</a>), TRTLLM GEN NVFP4 MoE with non-512-aligned hidden dims via weight padding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39510">#39510</a>), TRTLLM FP8 MoE with shuffled weights + BlockMajorK layout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38993">#38993</a>), fused qknorm+rope kernel on SM9.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37376">#37376</a>), tuned fused_moe config for RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39183">#39183</a>), ViT full CUDA graph for Qwen3-VL video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38061">#38061</a>), fused FP8 output quantization into <code>merge_attn_states</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36518">#36518</a>), batched KV-cache swap via <code>cuMemcpyBatchAsync</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38460">#38460</a>).</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>: ZenCPU / AMD Zen CPU backend via zentorch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39967">#39967</a>), RDNA 3.5/4 device IDs (gfx1150/1151/1201) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38455">#38455</a>), MORI EP for unquantized MoE with AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37529">#37529</a>), AITER gemm w8a8 ptpc integration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33773">#33773</a>), TritonW4A16LinearKernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37352">#37352</a>), asymmetric INT8 in <code>TritonInt8ScaledMMLinearKernel</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38501">#38501</a>), <code>fused_silu_mul_block_quant</code> enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38817">#38817</a>), KV-cache shuffle for <code>paged_attention_common</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32914">#32914</a>), MLA decode output zero-fill removed in AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37539">#37539</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Intel XPU</strong>: Initial GDN attention for Qwen3-Next / Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33657">#33657</a>), XPU MXFP8 quant op (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38682">#38682</a>), XPU MXFP4 quant op (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39857">#39857</a>), per-channel FP8 linear (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38316">#38316</a>), FP8 KV cache on XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37731">#37731</a>), <code>round_int8</code> for Intel Triton (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38825">#38825</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>: CPU draft-model speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32662">#32662</a>), CPU int8 compute mode in AWQ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35697">#35697</a>), head_size 512 in <code>cpu_attn</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38676">#38676</a>), gelu in <code>cpu_fused_moe</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38770">#38770</a>), OMP replacement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36487">#36487</a>), BF16 GELU LUT on ARM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37469">#37469</a>), W4A16 Autoround on CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38192">#38192</a>), CPU affinity/memory mgmt refactor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39781">#39781</a>), IBM Z s390x torch 2.11 builds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39910">#39910</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek / MLA / Indexer</strong>: Persistent TopK scheduler for DSV3.2 DSA decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37421">#37421</a>), DSV3.2 indexer fused weights projection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38684">#38684</a>), Triton MLA perf fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33529">#33529</a>), indexer WK upcast to BF16 for fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38928">#38928</a>), MLA indexer uniform-decode optimization for MTP&gt;1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39458">#39458</a>).</li>
<li><strong>GDN / Mamba</strong>: Kernel fusion in GDN (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37813">#37813</a>), TMA aligned with upstream FLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38981">#38981</a>), GPU↔CPU syncs eliminated in prefill and spec-decode paths (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38361">#38361</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38047">#38047</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Other</strong>: DeepGEMM integrated into the vLLM wheel via CMake (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37980">#37980</a>), Lustre FS checkpoint prefetching enabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39422">#39422</a>), Gemma4 fused routing Triton kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39083">#39083</a>), Gemma4 embed_input_ids GPU/CPU sync removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39234">#39234</a>), Nemotron VL image/video preprocessing optimized (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40283">#40283</a>), SiLU block-quant fusion v1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32996">#32996</a>), bilinear_pos_embed Triton kernel for ViT (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37948">#37948</a>), mean-pooling optimization (~5.9% throughput) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38559">#38559</a>), redundant-sync removal for pooling (~3.7% throughput) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39113">#39113</a>), H2D pageable-memory copy reduction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38794">#38794</a>), fused zero initializer for FP8 DeepGemm block-quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39547">#39547</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>EPLB</strong>: Alternative communication for EPLB weight exchange (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33176">#33176</a>), mapping optimization with router record for prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36261">#36261</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV Offload / Connector</strong>: 3FS KVConnector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37636">#37636</a>), unified memory layout for offloading workers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37206">#37206</a>), cache_salt propagated through MP connector for per-user isolation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39837">#39837</a>), multi-connector metrics of same type (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40010">#40010</a>), LMCache block-allocation event (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38856">#38856</a>), LMCache MP save optimization with MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38810">#38810</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Disaggregated / NIXL / Mamba</strong>: Heterogeneous TP 3-read conv-state transfer for NIXL + Mamba (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37635">#37635</a>), Nixl bumped to 0.10.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39922">#39922</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>New formats &amp; methods</strong>: TurboQuant 2-bit KV cache compression (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38479">#38479</a>), per-token-head INT8/FP8 KV cache quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38378">#38378</a>), fused FP8/NVFP4 output quantization in MLA attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/35792">#35792</a>), NVFP4 dense models on MI300/MI355X and Hopper via emulation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35733">#35733</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kernels</strong>: MXFP8 in Marlin GEMM/MoE with Mxfp8LinearOp refactor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34664">#34664</a>), MXFP4 W4A4 CUTLASS MoE for SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37463">#37463</a>), NVFP4 in <code>reshape_and_cache_flash</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37332">#37332</a>), batch-invariant NVFP4 linear (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39322">#39322</a>), FlashInfer CuteDSL batched-experts backend for NVFP4 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38251">#38251</a>), special <code>GptOssMxfp4MoeMethod</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39604">#39604</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Compressed tensors</strong>: W8A8 MXFP8 linear/MoE (<code>CompressedTensorsW8A8Mxfp8</code>) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38815">#38815</a>), layerwise reloading of attention/KV quantized models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38995">#38995</a>), experts_int8 consolidated with FP8 online quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38463">#38463</a>).</li>
<li><strong>XPU / CPU / AMD</strong>: XPU MXFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39857">#39857</a>), XPU MXFP8 GEMM + compressed-tensor schema (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38707">#38707</a>), XPU FP8 per-channel linear (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38316">#38316</a>), FP8 KV cache on XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37731">#37731</a>), CPU W4A16 Autoround (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38192">#38192</a>), XPU W4A16 Autoround (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37986">#37986</a>), asymmetric INT8 <code>TritonInt8ScaledMMLinearKernel</code> on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38501">#38501</a>), Quark W8A8 INT8 MoE inference (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36320">#36320</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Deprecations</strong>: Petit NVFP4 removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32694">#32694</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>OpenAI API</strong>: <code>presence_penalty</code> / <code>frequency_penalty</code> on Responses API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38613">#38613</a>), Responses API streaming migrated to unified parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38755">#38755</a>), Mistral Grammar factory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38150">#38150</a>), multimodal support on <code>/inference/v1/generate</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38405">#38405</a>), <code>max_tokens_per_doc</code> in rerank (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38827">#38827</a>), Generative Scoring (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34539">#34539</a>), MaxSim re-enabled on GPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38620">#38620</a>), auto-detection of <code>reasoning_config</code> when only <code>reasoning_parser</code> is set (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38214">#38214</a>), reasoning parsers can access model config via <code>adjust_request</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37848">#37848</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Pooling ecosystem</strong>: Pooling entrypoints overhauled across scoring (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28631">#28631</a>), pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39153">#39153</a>), and cleanup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39675">#39675</a>); preprocessing/postprocessing offloaded to thread pool (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39763">#39763</a>); async scheduling disabled by default for pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39592">#39592</a>); <code>logit_scale</code> added to PoolerConfig (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39435">#39435</a>), then renamed <code>logit_bias</code>/<code>logit_scale</code> → <code>logit_mean</code>/<code>logit_sigma</code> for affine score calibration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39530">#39530</a>) — breaking.</li>
<li><strong>gRPC / streaming</strong>: Streaming on token-generation endpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37171">#37171</a>); gRPC periodic stats logging + servicer log forwarding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38333">#38333</a>).</li>
<li><strong>LLM / CLI</strong>: Structured-output special tokens preserved in offline <code>LLM.chat</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39352">#39352</a>), <code>use_audio_in_video</code> passable at <code>vllm serve</code> for nemotron-nano-vl (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38538">#38538</a>), deferred imports save ~2s CLI startup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40056">#40056</a>), improved MM-input-too-long error message (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39409">#39409</a>), warning when FP8 KV cache misses prefill query quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39752">#39752</a>), clearer DCP error message (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28443">#28443</a>), <code>--model</code> deprecation warning updated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39518">#39518</a>), Mimo reasoning/tooling parsers mapped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40089">#40089</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>SSRF fix in batch runner <code>download_bytes_from_url</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38482">#38482</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>PyTorch 2.11</strong> for CUDA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34644">#34644</a>); XPU torch-xpu reverted to 2.10 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39656">#39656</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CUDA 13.0</strong> default with updated architecture lists and cleaned build-args (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39878">#39878</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlashAttention 4</strong> upstream sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38690">#38690</a>) and symlink-on-install behavior (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38814">#38814</a>).</li>
<li><strong>AITER</strong> triton BUFFER_OPS fix + version updates (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38580">#38580</a>), AITER reverted to v0.1.10.post3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39509">#39509</a>); <strong>Nixl</strong> bumped to 0.10.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39922">#39922</a>); <strong>DeepGEMM</strong> integrated into the wheel via CMake (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37980">#37980</a>); <strong>fastsafetensors</strong> added to NVIDIA Dockerfile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38950">#38950</a>); Helion bumped 0.3.2 → 0.3.3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38062">#38062</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Removed / moved</strong>: <code>resampy</code> dependency dropped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39524">#39524</a>), <code>librosa</code> direct dependency dropped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39079">#39079</a>), <code>pyav</code> and <code>soundfile</code> moved to common requirements (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39997">#39997</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Breaking Changes</h3>
<ol>
<li><strong>PyTorch 2.11 + CUDA 13.0 default</strong> — environment dependency change.</li>
<li><strong>Metrics rework</strong>: <code>vllm:prompt_tokens_recomputed</code> removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38709">#38709</a>); <code>num_cached_tokens</code> / <code>num_external_computed_tokens</code> replaced with <code>PrefillStats</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37460">#37460</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Pooler config rename</strong>: <code>logit_bias</code>/<code>logit_scale</code> → <code>logit_mean</code>/<code>logit_sigma</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39530">#39530</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Async scheduling default OFF for pooling models</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39592">#39592</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Petit NVFP4 quantization removed</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32694">#32694</a>); <code>cprofile</code> / <code>cprofile_context</code> deprecated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39100">#39100</a>); V0 <code>accept output buffer</code> deprecated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39125">#39125</a>).</li>
</ol>
<h3>V0 Deprecation</h3>
<ul>
<li>Petit NVFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32694">#32694</a>), accept output buffer in attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39125">#39125</a>), cprofile / cprofile_context (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39100">#39100</a>).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.19.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.19.1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>This is a patch release on top of <code>v0.19.0</code> with Transformers v5.5.3 upgrade and bug fixes for Gemma4:</p>
<ul>
<li>Update to transformers v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30566">#30566</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix] Fix invalid JSON in Gemma 4 streaming tool calls by stripping partial delimiters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38992">#38992</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix][Frontend] Fix Gemma4 streaming HTML duplication after tool calls (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38909">#38909</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix] Fix Gemma4 streaming tool call corruption for split boolean/number values (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39114">#39114</a>)</li>
<li>[Tool] adjust_request to reasoning parser, and Gemma4 fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39027">#39027</a>)</li>
<li>[Gemma4] Support quantized MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39045">#39045</a>)</li>
<li>Add Gemma4 Eagle3 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39450">#39450</a>)</li>
<li>[Gemma4][Bugfix]: Enable Gemma4ForCasualLM to load lora adapters correctly (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38844">#38844</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix] Fix Gemma4 tool parser converting bare null to string "null" (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39679">#39679</a>)</li>
<li>[Model] Fix Gemma 4 token repetition by dynamic BOS injection for PT models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39842">#39842</a>)</li>
<li>fix(kimi_k25): resolve media_placeholder_token_id from tokenizer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39344">#39344</a>)</li>
</ul>

### [v0.19.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.19.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.19.0</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 448 commits from 197 contributors (54 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Gemma 4 support</strong>: Full Google Gemma 4 architecture support including MoE, multimodal, reasoning, and tool-use capabilities (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38826">#38826</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38847">#38847</a>). Requires <code>transformers&gt;=5.5.0</code>. We recommend using pre-built docker image <code>vllm/vllm-openai:gemma4</code> for out of box usage.</li>
<li><strong>Zero-bubble async scheduling + speculative decoding</strong>: Async scheduling now supports speculative decoding with zero-bubble overlap, significantly improving throughput (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32951">#32951</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 maturation</strong>: MRV2 gains piecewise CUDA graphs for pipeline parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35162">#35162</a>), spec decode rejection sampler with greedy/logprobs support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37238">#37238</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37237">#37237</a>), multi-modal embeddings for spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36097">#36097</a>), streaming inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37028">#37028</a>), and EPLB support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37488">#37488</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ViT Full CUDA Graphs</strong>: Vision encoders (ViT) now support full CUDA graph capture for reduced overhead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35963">#35963</a>).</li>
<li><strong>General CPU KV cache offloading</strong>: A simple yet general CPU KV cache offloading mechanism for V1, with pluggable cache policy and block-level preemption handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37160">#37160</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37874">#37874</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34805">#34805</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36642">#36642</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37853">#37853</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DBO (Dual-Batch Overlap) generalization</strong>: The microbatch optimization (DBO) now works with general models, not just specific architectures (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37926">#37926</a>).</li>
<li><strong>NVIDIA B300/GB300 (SM 10.3) support</strong>: Allreduce fusion enabled by default with tuned all-reduce communicator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37755">#37755</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37756">#37756</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers v5 compatibility</strong>: Broad compatibility fixes across many models for HuggingFace Transformers v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37681">#37681</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38127">#38127</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38090">#38090</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38247">#38247</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38410">#38410</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>New architectures: Gemma 4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38826">#38826</a>), Cohere ASR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35809">#35809</a>), Cohere Transcribe (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38120">#38120</a>), ColQwen3.5 4.5B (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36887">#36887</a>), LFM2-ColBERT-350M (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37528">#37528</a>), Granite 4.0 1B Speech (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38019">#38019</a>), Qwen3-ForcedAligner (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35367">#35367</a>).</li>
<li>Speculative decoding: Eagle3 for Pixtral (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37182">#37182</a>), EagleMistralLarge3 fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37232">#37232</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA expansion: H2OVL tower/connector LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31696">#31696</a>), <code>--lora-target-modules</code> to restrict LoRA to specific modules (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34984">#34984</a>), <code>language_model_only</code> respected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37375">#37375</a>), Mistral3 fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36928">#36928</a>), Qwen3.5 fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36976">#36976</a>), out-of-tree ops replacement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37181">#37181</a>).</li>
<li>Model fixes: NemotronH MTP + Chunked Prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35447">#35447</a>), Qwen3-VL video timestamps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37439">#37439</a>), Qwen3.5 GDN quantized models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37448">#37448</a>), Qwen3Next A_log FP32 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37810">#37810</a>), JAIS ALiBi (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37820">#37820</a>), RoBERTa CUDA graph position IDs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37873">#37873</a>), AudioFlamingo3/MusicFlamingo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37643">#37643</a>), Music Flamingo loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35535">#35535</a>), bge-m3 task selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37632">#37632</a>), Nemotron Parse loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37407">#37407</a>), GLM OCR patch merger (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37962">#37962</a>), PaddleOCR checkpoint compat (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38232">#38232</a>), DeepSeek v3.2 params (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33703">#33703</a>), MiniMax NVFP4 weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37214">#37214</a>), gated model HF token (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37920">#37920</a>), Parakeet OOM on long audio (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36671">#36671</a>).</li>
<li>Features: Temporal compression for Nemotron-3-VL videos (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36808">#36808</a>), NemotronH Puzzle + MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37803">#37803</a>), torch.compile for InternVL vision encoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38049">#38049</a>), multiple embedding types in single call (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35829">#35829</a>).</li>
<li>Performance: GLM-4.xv ViT optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37779">#37779</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Zero-bubble async scheduling + speculative decoding</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32951">#32951</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: PP CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35162">#35162</a>), spec decode rejection sampler greedy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37238">#37238</a>) + logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37237">#37237</a>), multimodal embeddings for spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36097">#36097</a>), streaming inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37028">#37028</a>), configurable acceptance rate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38045">#38045</a>), FP32 draft logits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37526">#37526</a>), FP64 Gumbel noise (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37798">#37798</a>), warmup with spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37812">#37812</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ViT Full CUDA Graph</strong> capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35963">#35963</a>).</li>
<li><strong>General CPU KV cache offloading</strong> with pluggable CachePolicy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37160">#37160</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37874">#37874</a>), block-level preemption (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34805">#34805</a>), multiple KV groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36642">#36642</a>), hybrid model support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37853">#37853</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DBO for general models</strong>: Microbatch optimization generalized beyond specific architectures (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37926">#37926</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Compilation</strong>: Mega AOT artifact for torch 2.12+ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37198">#37198</a>), lazy graph module to defer recompile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37609">#37609</a>), remove model tag requirement for compile cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37345">#37345</a>), Triton autotuning disk cache enabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37188">#37188</a>), inductor runtime asserts disabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37485">#37485</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlexAttention</strong>: Custom mask modification support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37692">#37692</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Attention</strong>: Distinguish short extends vs decodes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37303">#37303</a>), allow qk_nope_head_dim=192 in FlashInfer MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37475">#37475</a>), skip sliding window attention layers with FP8 KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33695">#33695</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Scheduling</strong>: Schedule requests based on full input sequence length (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37307">#37307</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Spec decode</strong>: Per-draft-model MoE backend via <code>--speculative-config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37880">#37880</a>), Eagle3 drafter quant_config propagation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37280">#37280</a>), Eagle3 norm_before_fc propagation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38111">#38111</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Extensibility</strong>: PluggableLayer for CustomQwen2Decoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37293">#37293</a>), tensor IPC transfer for multimodal data (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32104">#32104</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: Optimize top-k in Triton sampler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37225">#37225</a>), optimize token_embed for pooling models with 1% improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37347">#37347</a>), fix slow hasattr in CUDAGraphWrapper (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37425">#37425</a>), NFS prefetch auto-enabled with RAM guard (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37673">#37673</a>), pybase64 replacement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37290">#37290</a>), optimize swap_states for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34733">#34733</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Bugfixes</strong>: Fix gibberish from FP8 MLA KV scale inconsistency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37054">#37054</a>), Mamba state corruption (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37728">#37728</a>), deadlock with pause/resume (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37024">#37024</a>), FlashInfer MNNVL socket collisions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36674">#36674</a>), multimodal prefix cache key collisions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36708">#36708</a>), DP coordinator ZMQ TOCTOU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37452">#37452</a>), CUDA graph memory double-counting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37426">#37426</a>), pooling non-determinism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37775">#37775</a>), AllReduce Fusion shutdown crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36955">#36955</a>), FlashInfer allreduce workspace (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37461">#37461</a>), async spec decoding with hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38556">#38556</a>), MLA sparse indexer prefill chunking (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36178">#36178</a>), KV offloading + MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37536">#37536</a>), async scheduling extra CUDA context (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37449">#37449</a>), DP MTP dummy run (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35243">#35243</a>), offloading+prefetch for GLM-4.7-FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37178">#37178</a>), max memory for multiple KV-cache groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36030">#36030</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>:
<ul>
<li>B300/GB300 (SM 10.3): Allreduce fusion enabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37755">#37755</a>), tuned all-reduce communicator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37756">#37756</a>).</li>
<li>Blackwell: Optimized SM120 CUTLASS blockwise FP8 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37970">#37970</a>), fix NVFP4 NaN on desktop Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37725">#37725</a>), fix DeepGEMM E8M0 accuracy for Qwen3.5 FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38083">#38083</a>), restore FP8 FlashMLA CUDA graph persistent buffers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35175">#35175</a>), DGX Spark fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38126">#38126</a>).</li>
<li>FlashInfer sparse MLA as default for FP8 KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37252">#37252</a>).</li>
<li>Tuned prefill configs for FP8 FA3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36265">#36265</a>), tuned Triton MoE config for Qwen3.5 on H200 with 9.9% E2E improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37340">#37340</a>), H800 MoE configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31201">#31201</a>).</li>
<li>GPT-OSS: Router GEMM kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37205">#37205</a>), eliminate padding with FlashInfer MXFP4/MXFP8 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30647">#30647</a>), reduce redundant SparseMatrix creation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37683">#37683</a>).</li>
<li>NVFP4 CUTLASS MoE non-gated support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37320">#37320</a>), fuse pack topk in TRTLLM MoE via torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37695">#37695</a>).</li>
<li>Non-contiguous KV cache in TRTLLM FP8 dequant kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36867">#36867</a>), Qwen3 dual stream input projection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36795">#36795</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>:
<ul>
<li>ROCm 7.2.1, torch 2.10, triton 3.6 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38252">#38252</a>).</li>
<li>DeepEP as all2all backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34692">#34692</a>).</li>
<li>Persistent MLA kernel from AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36574">#36574</a>), FP8xFP8 attention in AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36927">#36927</a>).</li>
<li>AWQ Marlin support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36505">#36505</a>), wvSplitK skinny GEMM for RDNA4/gfx1x (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34709">#34709</a>).</li>
<li>Nightly Docker image and wheel releases (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37283">#37283</a>).</li>
<li>Bugfixes: Sleep mode memory leak (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37533">#37533</a>), hybrid model stride (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37228">#37228</a>), qwen3_next crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36795">#36795</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Intel XPU</strong>: MLA model support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37143">#37143</a>), CompressedTensor W4A8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37207">#37207</a>), auto-detect XPU build platform (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37634">#37634</a>).</li>
<li><strong>TPU</strong>: Async scheduling interface (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36924">#36924</a>), Qwen3.5 FP8 weight loading fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37348">#37348</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>: Enable tcmalloc by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37607">#37607</a>), graceful degradation without tcmalloc/libiomp (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37561">#37561</a>), 48.9% throughput improvement for pooling models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38139">#38139</a>), OpenMP thread fix for torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37538">#37538</a>), structured output crash fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37706">#37706</a>), KV cache block zeroing crash fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37550">#37550</a>), slot mapping kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37987">#37987</a>), W4A16 compressed tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38219">#38219</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance fixes</strong>: FP8 DeepGEMM batch invariance (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37718">#37718</a>), Triton autotuning for Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37338">#37338</a>), TRTLLM NVFP4 routing precision (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36725">#36725</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Disaggregated serving</strong>: PD kv_transfer_params for Anthropic Messages (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37535">#37535</a>) and Responses API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37424">#37424</a>), Mooncake heterogeneous TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36869">#36869</a>), Mamba N-1 prefill for P/D (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37310">#37310</a>).</li>
<li><strong>EPLB</strong>: MRV2 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37488">#37488</a>), improved responsiveness (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36271">#36271</a>), EP weight filter fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37322">#37322</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Elastic EP</strong>: Fix repeated scale up/down cycles (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37131">#37131</a>), fix stateless group port races (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36330">#36330</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DBO</strong>: Generalized to work with all models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37926">#37926</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Multi-node</strong>: Fix allreduce fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38136">#38136</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV connector</strong>: Plugin-overridable metadata build (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37336">#37336</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Constraints</strong>: Cap API servers to 1 with Elastic EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37466">#37466</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Online MXFP8</strong> quantization for MoE and dense models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35448">#35448</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FP8</strong>: WoQ kernel abstraction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32929">#32929</a>), Marlin FP8 for compressed tensors fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38092">#38092</a>).</li>
<li><strong>NVFP4</strong>: Rescale weight scales to fix BF16 dequant underflow (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34577">#34577</a>), fix Marlin NaN/Inf with float16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33972">#33972</a>).</li>
<li><strong>QeRL</strong>: Online quantization composed with quantized reloading for RLHF (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38032">#38032</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>: W4A16 compressed tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38219">#38219</a>).</li>
<li><strong>XPU</strong>: CompressedTensor W4A8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37207">#37207</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ROCm</strong>: AWQ Marlin support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36505">#36505</a>).</li>
<li><strong>MXFP8 + DeepGEMM</strong>: Fix crash when both are active (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37358">#37358</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Removals</strong>: Per-tensor-per-channel FP8 removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32700">#32700</a>), Sparse24 integration and kernels removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36799">#36799</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>New endpoints</strong>: <code>/v1/chat/completions/batch</code> for batched chat completions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38011">#38011</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Features</strong>: Limit thinking tokens (hard limit) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20859">#20859</a>), multiple embedding types in single call (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35829">#35829</a>), numpy array embeddings for multimodal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38119">#38119</a>), <code>--lora-target-modules</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34984">#34984</a>), <code>-sc</code> shorthand for <code>--speculative-config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38380">#38380</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Tool parsing</strong>: GigaChat 3.1 parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36664">#36664</a>), Kimi-K2.5 reasoning/tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37438">#37438</a>), Gemma 4 tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38847">#38847</a>), tools passed to parser constructor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38029">#38029</a>), fix Mistral parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37209">#37209</a>), fix DeepSeek v3.2 streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36056">#36056</a>), fix GLM-4.7 parsing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37386">#37386</a>), fix Hermes streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38168">#38168</a>), fix OpenAI tool parser IndexError (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37958">#37958</a>), fix Anthropic streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37510">#37510</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Responses API</strong>: Fix crash with tool_choice=required exceeding max_output_tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37258">#37258</a>), fix TTFT recording (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37498">#37498</a>), fix Anthropic serving template kwargs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37899">#37899</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: Offload blocking tokenizer ops to thread pool (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34789">#34789</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Deprecations</strong>: <code>--calculate-kv-scales</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37201">#37201</a>), <code>score</code> task (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37537">#37537</a>), pooling multi-task support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37956">#37956</a>), <code>reasoning_content</code> message field removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37480">#37480</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Bugfixes</strong>: Embed/classify task routing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37573">#37573</a>), Cohere embed task instruction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38362">#38362</a>), renderer workers restricted to 1 with MM cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38418">#38418</a>).</li>
<li><strong>UX</strong>: Log once per node by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37568">#37568</a>), torch profiler with stack enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37571">#37571</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>Add <code>VLLM_MAX_N_SEQUENCES</code> environment variable to enforce sequence limits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37952">#37952</a>).</li>
<li>Enforce frame limit in VideoMediaIO to prevent resource exhaustion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38636">#38636</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li>Transformers v5 compatibility across many models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37681">#37681</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38127">#38127</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38247">#38247</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38410">#38410</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38090">#38090</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm 7.2.1, torch 2.10, triton 3.6 for ROCm builds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38252">#38252</a>).</li>
<li>compressed-tensors bumped to 0.14.0.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36988">#36988</a>).</li>
<li>Python OpenAI package bumped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32316">#32316</a>).</li>
<li>flashinfer-cubin added as default CUDA dependency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37233">#37233</a>).</li>
<li>librosa removed from audio dependencies (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37058">#37058</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>V0 Deprecation</h3>
<ul>
<li>Deprecate virtual engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37195">#37195</a>).</li>
<li>Deprecate <code>--disable-frontend-multiprocessing</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37612">#37612</a>).</li>
<li>Refactor KV cache from list to element (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37487">#37487</a>).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.18.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.18.1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>This is a patch release on top of v0.18.0 to address a few issues:</p>
<ul>
<li>Change default SM100 MLA prefill backend back to TRT-LLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38562">#38562</a>)</li>
<li>Fix mock.patch resolution failure for standalone_compile.FakeTensorMode on Python &lt;= 3.10 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37158">#37158</a>)</li>
<li>Disable monolithic TRTLLM MoE for Renormalize routing <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37605">#37605</a></li>
<li>Pre-download missing FlashInfer headers in Docker build <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38391">#38391</a></li>
<li>Fix DeepGemm E8M0 accuracy degradation for Qwen3.5 FP8 on Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38083">#38083</a>)</li>
</ul>

## Operating Systems

### [Ubuntu 26.04 LTS released](https://lwn.net/Articles/1069399/)

**来源**: LWN.net (Linux Weekly News)

**摘要**: <p>Ubuntu 26.04 ("Resolute Raccoon") LTS has been <a href="https://discourse.ubuntu.com/t/ubuntu-26-04-resolute-raccoon-lts-released/80833">released</a>
on schedule.</p>

<blockquote class="bq">
<p>This release brings a significant uplift in security, performance,
and usability across desktop, server, and cloud environments. Ubuntu
26.04 LTS introduces TPM-backed full-disk encryption, expanded use of
memory-safe components, improved application permission controls, and
Livepatch support for Arm systems, helping reduce downtime and
strengthen system resilience. [...]</p>

<p>The newest Edubuntu, Kubuntu, Lubuntu, Ubuntu Budgie, Ubuntu Cinnamon,
Ubuntu Kylin, Ubuntu Studio, Ubuntu Unity, and Xubuntu are also being
released today. For more details on these, read their individual release
notes under the Official flavors section:</p>

<p><a href="https://documentation.ubuntu.com/release-notes/26.04/#official-flavors">https://documentation.ubuntu.com/release-notes/26.04/#official-flavors</a></p>

<p>Maintenance updates will be provided for 5 years for Ubuntu Desktop, Ubuntu
Server, Ubuntu Cloud, Ubuntu WSL, and Ubuntu Core. All the remaining flavors
will be supported for 3 years.</p>
</blockquote>

<p>See the <a href="https://documentation.ubuntu.com/release-notes/26.04/">release
notes</a> for a list of changes, system requirements, and more.</p>

---

### [[$] Famfs, FUSE, and BPF](https://lwn.net/Articles/1068686/)

**来源**: LWN.net (Linux Weekly News)

**摘要**: The famfs filesystem first <a href="https://lwn.net/ml/all/cover.1708709155.git.john@groves.net/">showed up on the
mailing lists</a> in early 2024; since then, it has been the topic of
regular discussions at the Linux Storage, Filesystem, Memory Management and
BPF (LSFMM+BPF) Summit.  It has also, as result of those discussions, been
through some significant changes since that initial posting.  So it is not
surprising that a suggestion that it needed to be rewritten yet again was
not entirely well received.  How much more rewriting will actually be
needed is unclear, but more discussion appears certain.

### [Security updates for Thursday](https://lwn.net/Articles/1069356/)

**来源**: LWN.net (Linux Weekly News)

**摘要**: Security updates have been issued by <b>AlmaLinux</b> (kernel and osbuild-composer), <b>Debian</b> (cpp-httplib, firefox-esr, gimp, and packagekit), <b>Fedora</b> (chromium, composer, libcap, pgadmin4, pie, python3-docs, python3.14, and sudo), <b>Mageia</b> (gvfs), <b>Oracle</b> (.NET 8.0, delve, freerdp, giflib, ImageMagick, kernel, OpenEXR, and osbuild-composer), <b>SUSE</b> (erlang, giflib, google-guest-agent, GraphicsMagick, ignition, imagemagick, kea, kernel, kissfft, libraw, libssh, ocaml-patch, opam, openCryptoki, openexr, openssl-1_1, tomcat, tomcat10, tomcat11, and tor), and <b>Ubuntu</b> (linux, linux-aws, linux-aws-5.4, linux-azure, linux-gcp, linux-gcp-5.4,
 linux-hwe-5.4, linux-ibm, linux-ibm-5.4, linux-iot, linux-kvm,
 linux-oracle, linux-oracle-5.4, linux-xilinx-zynqmp, linux-aws, linux-aws-6.17, linux-hwe-6.17, linux-oracle, linux-oracle-6.17, linux-azure, linux-intel-iotg, linux-intel-iotg-5.15, linux-kvm, linux-oracle-5.15, linux-azure-5.4, linux-azure-fips, linux-fips, linux-aws-fips, linux-azure-fips, linux-gcp-fips, linux-hwe-6.8, linux-ibm-6.8, linux-raspi, linux-oracle, linux-oracle-6.8, linux-raspi, linux-raspi-5.4, linux-raspi-realtime, packagekit, python-tornado, ruby-rack-session, slurm-llnl, and strongswan).
